# GSQ224 changed-projection export

The fixed distribution screen in [[sources/runs/2026/10/2026-10-06-gsq224-source-and-quality]] passed before this export was authorized by its prospective protocol. The export completes in 55.238872 seconds with a 242,844,440-byte lifetime physical peak, under the fixed four-GB producer limit, thirteen-GB actual preflight, continuing three-GB headroom and normal pressure. The 430-GB allocated research cap includes a prospective 200-MB allowance for the bounded native follow-up. Subsequent native parity uses the existing six-input-token and sixteen-self-fed-step fixtures, and completed tasks retain the existing sixteen-case grader and advance condition. No quality or throughput claim follows from export duration.
